 105:     /** FIX-127: existing decision order; invoked inside the coordinator's transaction. */
 106:     private Optional<PaperPosition> processSignalBody(TradeSignal signal) {
 107:         if (signal == null) {
 108:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Trade signal is required");
 109:         }
 110: 
 111:         String symbol = normalizeSymbol(signal.getSymbol());
 112: 
 113:         // FIX-127: position management persists advice and can request POSITION_STOP_LOSS.
 114:         // Keep its existing decision order, but acquire the initial managed lock before entering here.
 115:         if (positionManagementService != null) {
 116:             try {
 117:                 positionManagementService.analyze(signal);
 118:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 119:                 log.warn("Position advisory failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage());
 120:             }
 121:         }
 122:         Optional<PaperPosition> openPosition = positionRepository
 123:                 .findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN);
 124: 
 125:         if (openPosition.isPresent()) {
 126:             PaperPosition position = openPosition.get();
 127:             BigDecimal price = signal.getLatestPrice();
 128: 
 129:             // Never treat a delayed timeframe candle close as the current mark price.
 130:             // Context from that signal is still processed below, but TP/SL/profit-lock
 131:             // mechanics require a post-entry market observation. Live production protection
 132:             // is independently driven by LivePositionProtectionService.onPrice(...).
 133:             boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(signal, position.getOpenedAt());
 134:             DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation profitLock = authoritativePrice
 135:                     ? dynamicProfitLockService.evaluate(signal)
 136:                     : DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation.inactive(
 137:                             "Signal price predates the open position and is context-only for mechanical protection.");
 138: 
 139:             // FIX-118 Production trace for the independent signal-price Profit Lock authority.
 140:             // The explicit ProfitLockState now also shows whether the historical lock is ACTIVE
 141:             // or temporarily non-executable while TP extension is rebasing.
 142:             if (authoritativePrice && (profitLock.active() || profitLock.triggered() || profitLock.rebasing())) {
 143:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_PATH] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, price={}, " +
 144:                                 "active={}, progressPct={}, activationPct={}, lock={}, state={}, triggered={}",
 145:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, signal.getInterval(), price,
 146:                         profitLock.active(), profitLock.progressPercent(), profitLock.activationPercent(),
 147:                         profitLock.lockPrice(), profitLock.state(), profitLock.triggered());
 148:             }
 149: 
 150:             // FIX-067: The wallet-managed position is the authoritative Production source for
 151:             // a dynamically extended take-profit.  paper_position historically retained the
 152:             // entry-time TP, so a fresh 1m signal could close against that stale threshold even
 153:             // after LivePositionProtectionService had persisted TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED.
 154:             // Prefer the OPEN managed TP and repair the paper snapshot when they differ. This is
 155:             // deliberately NOT a TP-policy change: it only makes both existing exit paths compare
 156:             // against the same already-approved target.
 157:             BigDecimal authoritativeTakeProfit = position.getTakeProfit();
 158:             WalletManagedPosition managedPosition = walletManagedPositionRepository
 159:                     .findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
 160:                     .orElse(null);
 161:             if (managedPosition != null && managedPosition.getTakeProfitUsdt() != null) {
 162:                 authoritativeTakeProfit = managedPosition.getTakeProfitUsdt();
 163:                 if (position.getTakeProfit() == null
 164:                         || position.getTakeProfit().compareTo(authoritativeTakeProfit) != 0) {
 165:                     position.setTakeProfit(authoritativeTakeProfit);
 166:                     positionRepository.save(position);
 167:                 }
 168:             }
 169: 
 170:             if (authoritativePrice && authoritativeTakeProfit != null
 171:                     && price.compareTo(authoritativeTakeProfit) >= 0) {
 172:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 173:                         "TAKE_PROFIT", "Price reached the authoritative configured take-profit target."));
 174:             }
 175: 
 176:             if (authoritativePrice && profitLock.triggered()) {
 177:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_TRIGGER] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, progressPct={}",
 178:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, profitLock.lockPrice(), profitLock.progressPercent());
 179:                 return Optional.of(closeFromProfitLock(position, signal, profitLock));
 180:             }
 181: 
 182:             if (authoritativePrice && price.compareTo(position.getStopLoss()) <= 0) {
 183:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.STOPPED,
 184:                         "STOP_LOSS", "Price reached the configured stop loss."));
 185:             }
 186: 
 187:             // FIX-118: in TP_EXTENSION_REBASE the old Profit Lock cannot execute, but the
 188:             // direct protected-profit floor still applies. Stop Loss remains absolute and
 189:             // therefore keeps priority immediately above this branch.
 190:             if (authoritativePrice && profitLock.hardProfitFloorTriggered()) {
 191:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, floor={}",
 192:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, profitLock.hardProfitFloor());
 193:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 194:                         "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", profitLock.explanation()));
 195:             }
 196: 
 197:             if (signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.SELL
 198:                     || signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) {
 199:                 if (profitLock.active()) {
 200:                     log.info("Normal wallet SELL suppressed because Dynamic Profit Lock is active: signalId={}, symbol={}, protectedPrice={}, currentPrice={}",
 201:                             signal.getId(), signal.getSymbol(), profitLock.lockPrice(), signal.getLatestPrice());
 202:                     return Optional.of(position);
 203:                 }
 204:                 TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validation =
 205:                         executionValidationService.validateSell(signal);
 206:                 if (validation.allowed()) {
 207:                     return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 208:                             signal.getDecision().name(), signal.getExplanation()));
 209:                 }
 210:                 log.info("Normal wallet SELL rejected: signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, reason={}, detail={}",
 211:                         signal.getId(), signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(),
 212:                         validation.code(), validation.explanation());
 213:             }
 214: 
 215:             // Progressive Position Building may add to an existing position only after
 216:             // TP / Profit Lock / SL / validated SELL logic had first priority.
 217:             if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && !profitLock.active()) {
 218:                 WalletManagedPosition managed = walletManagedPositionRepository
 219:                         .findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
 220:                         .orElse(null);
 221:                 if (managed != null) {
 222:                     ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision addDecision =
 223:                             executionIntelligenceService.evaluateBuy(
 224:                                     signal,
 225:                                     managed.getAllocatedPositionPercent(),
 226:                                     managed.getEntryStage());
 227:                     if (addDecision.allowed()) {
 228:                         if (walletTradeRepository
 229:                                 .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(
 230:                                         signal.getId(), "BUY", "EXECUTED")
 231:                                 .isPresent()) {
 232:                             return Optional.of(position);
 233:                         }
 234:                         int addPercent = addDecision.positionPercent();
 235:                         // FIX-056: progressive adds use the same fresh canonical price authority
 236:                         // as initial entries. Signal.latestPrice remains decision-time evidence only.
 237:                         ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh = executionPriceAuthorityService
 238:                                 .resolve(symbol, Instant.now()).orElse(null);
 239:                         if (fresh == null) {
 240:                             log.info("Progressive BUY skipped: no fresh execution price for {}", symbol);
 241:                             return Optional.of(position);
 242:                         }
 243:                         ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision freshDecision =
 244:                                 executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(signal, addDecision, fresh.price());
 245:                         if (!freshDecision.allowed()) return Optional.of(position);
 246:                         addPercent = freshDecision.positionPercent();
 247:                         ExecutionIntelligenceService.EntryQuality entryQuality =
 248:                                 executionIntelligenceService.assessEntryQualityAtPrice(signal, fresh.price());
 249:                         boolean walletAdded = walletAutoExecutionService.executeBuy(
 250:                                 signal, fresh.price(), fresh.observedAt(), addPercent,
 251:                                 "Execution Intelligence [" + freshDecision.source() + "] " + freshDecision.explanation()
 252:                                         + " FreshPrice=" + fresh.price() + "@" + fresh.observedAt(),
 253:                                 freshDecision.source(),
 254:                                 entryQuality.score());
 255:                         if (walletAdded) {
 256:                             BigDecimal addedQuantity = walletTradeRepository
 257:                                     .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(
 258:                                             signal.getId(), "BUY", "EXECUTED")
 259:                                     .map(com.crypto.wallet.domain.WalletTrade::getQuantity)
 260:                                     .filter(q -> q != null && q.signum() > 0)
 261:                                     .orElse(BigDecimal.ZERO);
 262:                             if (addedQuantity.signum() > 0) {
 263:                                 BigDecimal oldCost = position.getEntryPrice().multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 264:                                 BigDecimal addCost = fresh.price().multiply(addedQuantity, MC);
 265:                                 BigDecimal newQuantity = position.getQuantity().add(addedQuantity);
 266:                                 position.setQuantity(newQuantity);
 267:                                 position.setEntryPrice(oldCost.add(addCost, MC).divide(newQuantity, MC));
 268:                                 if (signal.getStopLoss() != null
 269:                                         && signal.getStopLoss().compareTo(position.getStopLoss()) > 0) {
 270:                                     position.setStopLoss(signal.getStopLoss());
 271:                                 }
 272:                                 if (signal.getTakeProfit() != null
 273:                                         && signal.getTakeProfit().compareTo(position.getTakeProfit()) > 0) {
 274:                                     position.setTakeProfit(signal.getTakeProfit());
 275:                                 }
 276:                                 position.setEntryReason(position.getEntryReason()
 277:                                         + " | Progressive add [" + addDecision.source() + "] "
 278:                                         + addPercent + "% at " + fresh.price()
 279:                                         + "; Entry Quality=" + entryQuality.score() + "/100.");
 280:                                 positionRepository.save(position);
 281:                             }
 282:                             executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(signal, addDecision);
 283:                         }
 284:                     }
 285:                 }
 286:             }
 287: 
 288:             // Non-execution timeframes and non-executable decisions only update context/advisory state.
 289:             return Optional.of(position);
 290:         }

 292:         // FIX-014: a fresh 5m BUY transition may wake an already-live deferred 1m BUY
 293:         // opportunity, but 5m never executes directly. The returned executionSignal is the
 294:         // latest fresh 1m timing/risk plan; all existing SETUP_TIMEFRAME_ATR and hard-risk
 295:         // guards remain authoritative. Normal 1m BUY handling is unchanged.
 296:         TradeSignal executionSignal = signal;
 297:         ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision executionDecision;
 298:         ExecutionIntelligenceService.SetupWakeupEvaluation wakeup =
 299:                 executionIntelligenceService.evaluateSetupTimeframeWakeup(signal, 0);
 300:         if (!wakeup.present()) {
 301:             // FIX-023: a fresh executable 5m BUY transition may also wake an existing
 302:             // opportunity after liquidity/confirmation improves. The returned signal is
 303:             // still the latest 1m timing/risk plan; 5m never executes independently.
 304:             wakeup = executionIntelligenceService.evaluateConfirmedSetupWakeup(signal, 0);
 305:         }
 306:         if (wakeup.present() && wakeup.decision().allowed()) {
 307:             executionSignal = wakeup.executionSignal();
 308:             executionDecision = wakeup.decision();
 309:         } else {

 311:         }
 312: 
 313:         if (!executionDecision.allowed()) {
 314:             log.info("Execution Intelligence did not open a BUY: signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, state={}, source={}, code={}, evidenceScore={}, buys={}, watches={}, detail={}",
 315:                     signal.getId(), signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(), executionDecision.state(),
 316:                     executionDecision.source(), executionDecision.code(),
 317:                     executionDecision.evidence().evidenceScore(), executionDecision.evidence().buyCount(),
 318:                     executionDecision.evidence().watchCount(), executionDecision.explanation());
 319:             return Optional.empty();
 320:         }
 321: 
 322:         if (positionRepository.countByStatus(PositionStatus.OPEN) >= properties.maxOpenPositions()) {
 323:             return Optional.empty();
 324:         }
 325: 
 326:         enforceDailyLossLimit();
 327: 
 328:         int atrPercent = executionSignal.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent() <= 0
 329:                 ? 100
 330:                 : executionSignal.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent();
 331:         int executionPercent = executionDecision.positionPercent();
 332:         int effectivePositionPercent = Math.max(1,
 333:                 Math.min(100, (int) Math.round(atrPercent * executionPercent / 100.0)));
 334:         BigDecimal positionScale = BigDecimal.valueOf(effectivePositionPercent)
 335:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC);
 336:         BigDecimal riskAmount = properties.paperAccountBalance()
 337:                 .multiply(properties.riskPerTradePercent(), MC)
 338:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 339:                 .multiply(positionScale, MC);
 340:         // FIX-056: resolve a canonical execution-time price AFTER all signal/HTF gates but
 341:         // BEFORE risk sizing and wallet persistence. Never size from a stale signal snapshot.
 342:         ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice freshExecutionPrice = executionPriceAuthorityService
 343:                 .resolve(symbol, Instant.now()).orElse(null);
 344:         if (freshExecutionPrice == null) {
 345:             log.info("Execution Intelligence approved signal {} but no fresh canonical execution price was available; BUY skipped.",
 346:                     executionSignal.getId());
 347:             return Optional.empty();
 348:         }
 349:         executionDecision = executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(
 350:                 executionSignal, executionDecision, freshExecutionPrice.price());
 351:         if (!executionDecision.allowed()) {
 352:             log.info("BUY revalidation rejected signal {} at fresh price {}: {} - {}",
 353:                     executionSignal.getId(), freshExecutionPrice.price(), executionDecision.code(), executionDecision.explanation());
 354:             return Optional.empty();
 355:         }
 356:         // Recompute exposure after fresh-price Entry Quality because the final guard may
 357:         // reduce the approved allocation further than the signal-time decision did.
 358:         executionPercent = executionDecision.positionPercent();
 359:         effectivePositionPercent = Math.max(1,
 360:                 Math.min(100, (int) Math.round(atrPercent * executionPercent / 100.0)));
 361:         positionScale = BigDecimal.valueOf(effectivePositionPercent)
 362:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC);
 363:         riskAmount = properties.paperAccountBalance()
 364:                 .multiply(properties.riskPerTradePercent(), MC)
 365:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 366:                 .multiply(positionScale, MC);
 367:         BigDecimal riskPerUnit = freshExecutionPrice.price().subtract(executionSignal.getStopLoss(), MC).abs();
 368: 
 369:         if (riskPerUnit.signum() == 0) {
 370:             throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid stop-loss distance");
 371:         }
 372: 
 373:         BigDecimal riskModelQuantity = riskAmount.divide(riskPerUnit, MC);
 374: 
 375:         final boolean walletExecuted;
 376:         try {
 377:             ExecutionIntelligenceService.EntryQuality entryQuality =
 378:                     executionIntelligenceService.assessEntryQualityAtPrice(executionSignal, freshExecutionPrice.price());
 379:             walletExecuted = walletAutoExecutionService.executeBuy(executionSignal, freshExecutionPrice.price(), freshExecutionPrice.observedAt(), effectivePositionPercent,
 380:                     "Execution Intelligence [" + executionDecision.source() + "] " + executionDecision.explanation()
 381:                             + " DecisionPrice=" + executionSignal.getLatestPrice()
 382:                             + ", executionPrice=" + freshExecutionPrice.price()
 383:                             + ", priceObservedAt=" + freshExecutionPrice.observedAt(),
 384:                     executionDecision.source(),
 385:                     entryQuality.score());
 386:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 387:             log.error("Automatic wallet BUY failed for signal {}: {}", executionSignal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
 388:             return Optional.empty();
 389:         }
 390:         if (!walletExecuted) {
 391:             log.info("Execution Intelligence approved signal {} but wallet controls declined execution; no paper position was created.", executionSignal.getId());
 392:             return Optional.empty();
 393:         }
 394: 
 395:         BigDecimal executedQuantity = walletTradeRepository
 396:                 .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(executionSignal.getId(), "BUY", "EXECUTED")
 397:                 .map(com.crypto.wallet.domain.WalletTrade::getQuantity)
 398:                 .filter(q -> q != null && q.signum() > 0)
 399:                 .orElse(riskModelQuantity);
 400: 
 401:         executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(executionSignal, executionDecision);
 402:         PaperPosition position = positionRepository.save(PaperPosition.builder()
 403:                 .symbol(symbol)
 404:                 .side(PositionSide.BUY)
 405:                 .status(PositionStatus.OPEN)
 406:                 .quantity(executedQuantity)
 407:                 .entryPrice(freshExecutionPrice.price())
 408:                 .stopLoss(executionSignal.getStopLoss())
 409:                 .takeProfit(executionSignal.getTakeProfit())
 410:                 .signal(executionSignal)
 411:                 .entryReason(executionSignal.getExplanation() + " | Execution Intelligence [" + executionDecision.source() + "]: " + executionDecision.explanation() + " Effective position " + effectivePositionPercent + "%.")
 412:                 .openedAt(Instant.now())
 413:                 .build());
 414:         return Optional.of(position);
 415:     }

 423:     @Transactional
 424:     public PaperPosition close(Long positionId, BigDecimal exitPrice) {
 425:         PaperPosition position = positionRepository.findById(positionId)
 426:                 .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Position not found"));
 427: 
 428:         if (position.getStatus() != PositionStatus.OPEN) {
 429:             throw new IllegalStateException("Position is already closed");
 430:         }
 431: 
 432:         return completeClose(position, exitPrice,
 433:                 exitPrice.compareTo(position.getStopLoss()) <= 0 ? PositionStatus.STOPPED : PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 434:                 "MANUAL_CLOSE", "Position was manually closed.", null);
 435:     }

 452:     private PaperPosition closeFromProfitLock(
 453:             PaperPosition position,
 454:             TradeSignal signal,
 455:             DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation profitLock
 456:     ) {
 457:         BigDecimal exitPrice = signal.getLatestPrice();
 458:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
 459:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 460:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
 461:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
 462:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 463: 
 464:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 465:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 466:         position.setStatus(PositionStatus.CLOSED);
 467:         position.setCloseReason("PROFIT_LOCK");
 468:         position.setExitReason(profitLock.explanation());
 469:         position.setExitSignal(signal);
 470:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 471:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
 472: 
 473:         // FIX-020: terminal position close consumes the opportunity that financed this
 474:         // position so old pre-exit evidence cannot immediately open a brand-new trade.
 475:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), signal, "PROFIT_LOCK");
 476: 
 477:         try {
 478:             walletAutoExecutionService.executeProfitLock(signal, exitPrice, profitLock.lockPrice());
 479:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 480:             log.error("Dynamic Profit Lock wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
 481:         }
 482:         // FIX-028: profit-lock exits use the same immutable production audit trail.
 483:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, signal, "PROFIT_LOCK", profitLock.explanation());
 484:         return saved;
 485:     }
 486: 
 487:     private PaperPosition completeClose(
 488:             PaperPosition position,
 489:             BigDecimal exitPrice,
 490:             PositionStatus status,
 491:             String closeReason,
 492:             String explanation,
 493:             TradeSignal exitSignal
 494:     ) {
 495:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
 496:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 497:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
 498:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
 499:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 500: 
 501:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 502:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 503:         position.setStatus(status);
 504:         position.setCloseReason(closeReason);
 505:         position.setExitReason(explanation);
 506:         position.setExitSignal(exitSignal);
 507:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 508:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
 509: 
 510:         // FIX-020: all terminal exits (TP/SL/SIGNAL_SELL/manual/etc.) create an immutable
 511:         // evidence boundary. Progressive adds remain untouched while the position is open.
 512:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), exitSignal, closeReason);
 513: 
 514:         if (exitSignal != null) {
 515:             try {
 516:                 // FIX-028: keep execution behavior identical, but preserve the TRUE terminal
 517:                 // reason in wallet history. A TAKE_PROFIT/STOP_LOSS may use the latest 1m
 518:                 // signal as its price/context carrier even when that signal is WATCH. Calling
 519:                 // executeSell(...) used to mislabel those exits as SIGNAL_SELL.
 520:                 if ("SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason) || "STRONG_SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason)) {
 521:                     walletAutoExecutionService.executeSell(exitSignal);
 522:                 } else {
 523:                     walletAutoExecutionService.executeSignalLinkedExit(exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 524:                 }
 525:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 526:                 log.error("Automatic wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", exitSignal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
 527:             }
 528:         }
 529: 
 530:         // FIX-028: immutable production audit records the actual close trigger separately
 531:         // from the latest market signal. This is diagnostic only and cannot alter execution.
 532:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 533:         return saved;
 534:     }
 535: 
 536:     private String normalizeSymbol(String symbol) {
 537:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) {
 538:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
 539:         }
 540:         return symbol.trim().toUpperCase();
 541:     }
 542: 
 543:     private void enforceDailyLossLimit() {
 544:         Instant startOfDay = LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC).atStartOfDay().toInstant(ZoneOffset.UTC);
 545:         BigDecimal pnl = positionRepository.sumRealizedPnlSince(startOfDay);
 546:         BigDecimal maximumLoss = properties.paperAccountBalance()
 547:                 .multiply(properties.maxDailyLossPercent(), MC)
 548:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 549:                 .negate();
 550: 
 551:         if (pnl.compareTo(maximumLoss) <= 0) {
 552:             throw new IllegalStateException("Daily loss limit reached");
 553:         }
 554:     }
 555: }

 139:     private BigDecimal decimal(
 140:             JsonNode node,
 141:             String field
 142:     ) {
 143:         String value = node.path(field).asText();
 144: 
 145:         if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
 146:             return BigDecimal.ZERO;
 147:         }
 148: 
 149:         return new BigDecimal(value);

 251:     private void evaluateNearTpProtection(WalletManagedPosition managed, BigDecimal price,
 252:                                           TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
 253:         // FIX-11T evidence is explicitly time-safe. Normal Production logic above keeps its
 254:         // existing signal lookup unchanged; Near-TP alone falls back to an at-or-before query
 255:         // if the repository's newest row is future-dated relative to this live evaluation.
 256:         Instant evaluatedAt = Instant.now();
 257:         oneMinute = latestEligibleSignal(managed.getSymbol(), "1m", oneMinute, evaluatedAt);
 258:         fiveMinute = latestEligibleSignal(managed.getSymbol(), "5m", fiveMinute, evaluatedAt);
 259: 
 260:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 261:                 managed.getNearTpState() == null ? NearTpState.INACTIVE : managed.getNearTpState(),
 262:                 managed.getNearTpBestPrice(), managed.getNearTpBearishStreak(),
 263:                 managed.getNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), managed.isNearTpHarvestUsed());
 264: 
 265:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation evaluation = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
 266:                 state, managed.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt(),
 267:                 price, evaluatedAt, oneMinute, fiveMinute);
 268: 
 269:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State next = evaluation.state();
 270:         boolean stateChanged = managed.getNearTpState() != next.nearTpState()
 271:                 || !sameDecimal(managed.getNearTpBestPrice(), next.bestPrice())
 272:                 || managed.getNearTpBearishStreak() != next.consecutiveBearishOneMinute()
 273:                 || !java.util.Objects.equals(managed.getNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), next.lastEvaluatedOneMinuteSignalId());
 274:         if (stateChanged) {
 275:             managed.setNearTpState(next.nearTpState());
 276:             managed.setNearTpBestPrice(next.bestPrice());
 277:             managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(next.consecutiveBearishOneMinute());
 278:             managed.setNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(next.lastEvaluatedOneMinuteSignalId());
 279:             managed.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 280:             managedPositionRepository.save(managed);
 281:         }
 282: 
 283:         if (evaluation.transition() || stateChanged) {
 284:             log.info("FIX-11T Near-TP evaluation: positionId={}, symbol={}, state={}, code={}, price={}, bestPrice={}, " +
 285:                             "tpProgressPct={}, givebackPct={}, bearish1mStreak={}, oneMinuteId={}, oneMinuteOriginal={}, " +
 286:                             "fiveMinuteId={}, fiveMinuteOriginal={}, detail={}",
 287:                     managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), next.nearTpState(), evaluation.code(), price, next.bestPrice(),
 288:                     evaluation.tpProgressPercent(), evaluation.givebackPercent(), next.consecutiveBearishOneMinute(),
 289:                     oneMinute == null ? null : oneMinute.getId(),
 290:                     oneMinute == null ? null : oneMinute.getOriginalDecision(),
 291:                     fiveMinute == null ? null : fiveMinute.getId(),
 292:                     fiveMinute == null ? null : fiveMinute.getOriginalDecision(),
 293:                     evaluation.explanation());
 294:         } else if (log.isDebugEnabled() && evaluation.code().startsWith("HOLD_")) {
 295:             log.debug("FIX-11T Near-TP hold: positionId={}, symbol={}, code={}, price={}, detail={}",
 296:                     managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), evaluation.code(), price, evaluation.explanation());
 297:         }
 298: 
 299:         if (!evaluation.harvestEligible()) return;
 300: 
 301:         WalletAutoExecutionService.PartialHarvestResult harvest = walletAutoExecutionService
 302:                 .executeNearTpPartialHarvest(managed.getSymbol(), price, evaluation.explanation());
 303:         if (!harvest.executed()) {
 304:             log.warn("FIX-11T Near-TP harvest not executed: positionId={}, symbol={}, status={}, price={}",
 305:                     managed.getId(), managed.getSymbol(), harvest.status(), price);
 306:             // If execution did not happen, return to rejection monitoring rather than leaving
 307:             // the state stranded in FAILURE_CONFIRMED. Idempotency still lives in wallet_trade.
 308:             managed.setNearTpState(NearTpState.NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED);
 309:             if ("BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM".equals(harvest.status())
 310:                     || "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE".equals(harvest.status())) {
 311:                 // The skipped order consumed the current confirmation pair. Requiring fresh
 312:                 // bearish evidence prevents a retry on every subsequent market-price tick.
 313:                 managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(0);
 314:             }
 315:             managed.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 316:             managedPositionRepository.save(managed);
 317:             return;
 318:         }
 319: 
 320:         // Keep paper_position quantity/P&L synchronized with the managed wallet lifecycle.
 321:         // Without this, a later terminal signal close would calculate P&L on pre-harvest quantity.
 322:         PaperPosition paper = paperPositionRepository.findBySymbolAndStatus(managed.getSymbol(), PositionStatus.OPEN).orElse(null);
 323:         if (paper != null) {
 324:             paper.setQuantity(harvest.remainingQuantity());
 325:             BigDecimal priorRealized = paper.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : paper.getRealizedPnl();
 326:             paper.setRealizedPnl(priorRealized.add(harvest.realizedPnlUsdt(), MC));
 327:             paperPositionRepository.save(paper);
 328:         }

 336:     private TradeSignal latestEligibleSignal(String symbol, String interval, TradeSignal newest, Instant evaluatedAt) {
 337:         if (newest != null && newest.getGeneratedAt() != null && !newest.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(evaluatedAt)) {
 338:             return newest;
 339:         }
 340:         return tradeSignalRepository

 345:     /**
 346:      * FIX-117: TP extension must not erase Near-TP protection that was already earned.
 347:      *
 348:      * Only ARMED and REJECTION_DETECTED change from the pre-FIX-117 behavior. INACTIVE
 349:      * still resets against the new geometry. FAILURE_CONFIRMED also keeps the existing
 350:      * reset behavior because that persisted/transient lifecycle has a real interruption
 351:      * window between state save and harvest resolution and is intentionally out of scope.
 352:      * PARTIAL_HARVESTED remains terminal through the existing harvest-used guard.
 353:      *
 354:      * plannedDistance is recalculated from the current TP by NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy
 355:      * on every evaluation, so the historical best price remains a valid market observation
 356:      * and does not need synthetic rebasing after an extension.
 357:      */
 358:     private void resetNearTpTrackingForNewRiskGeometry(WalletManagedPosition managed) {
 359:         if (managed.isNearTpHarvestUsed()) return;
 360: 
 361:         NearTpState currentState = managed.getNearTpState() == null
 362:                 ? NearTpState.INACTIVE : managed.getNearTpState();
 363:         switch (currentState) {
 364:             case INACTIVE, NEAR_TP_FAILURE_CONFIRMED -> {
 365:                 // Existing behavior: no earned protection is carried into the new geometry.
 366:                 managed.setNearTpState(NearTpState.INACTIVE);
 367:                 managed.setNearTpBestPrice(null);
 368:                 managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(0);
 369:                 managed.setNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(null);
 370:             }
 371:             case NEAR_TP_ARMED -> {
 372:                 // FIX-117: preserve earned protection and the real historical best price.
 373:             }
 374:             case NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED -> {
 375:                 // Preserve earned protection/best price, but discard bearish persistence
 376:                 // accumulated under the old, tighter geometry. The next evaluation will
 377:                 // recalculate giveback against the new TP and either recover or remain rejected.
 378:                 managed.setNearTpBearishStreak(0);
 379:                 managed.setNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(null);
 380:             }
 381:             case NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED -> {
 382:                 // Existing terminal behavior; normally returned above by nearTpHarvestUsed.
 383:             }
 384:         }
 385:     }

 387:     private boolean sameDecimal(BigDecimal a, BigDecimal b) {
 388:         return a == null ? b == null : b != null && a.compareTo(b) == 0;
 389:     }

 391:     private void closePaper(String symbol, BigDecimal exitPrice, PositionStatus status,
 392:                             String closeReason, String explanation, TradeSignal sourceSignal) {
 393:         PaperPosition paper = paperPositionRepository.findBySymbolAndStatus(symbol, PositionStatus.OPEN).orElse(null);
 394:         if (paper == null) return;
 395:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(paper.getEntryPrice(), MC).multiply(paper.getQuantity(), MC);
 396:         // FIX-11T: paper_position may already contain realized P&L from a Near-TP partial
 397:         // harvest. Preserve it when the remaining quantity is later closed.
 398:         BigDecimal pnl = (paper.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : paper.getRealizedPnl())
 399:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 400:         paper.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 401:         paper.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 402:         paper.setStatus(status);
 403:         paper.setCloseReason(closeReason);
 404:         paper.setExitReason(explanation);
 405:         paper.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 406:         PaperPosition saved = paperPositionRepository.save(paper);
 407: 
 408:         // FIX-028: live-price exits (TP/SL/Profit Lock/normal exit) also write the
 409:         // immutable audit record. sourceSignal is context only unless the closeTrigger
 410:         // itself is a genuine SELL decision. No protection/exit policy is changed here.
 411:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, sourceSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 412:     }

 456:     ) {
 457:         BigDecimal exitPrice = signal.getLatestPrice();
 458:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
 459:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 460:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
 461:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
 462:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 463: 
 464:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 465:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 466:         position.setStatus(PositionStatus.CLOSED);
 467:         position.setCloseReason("PROFIT_LOCK");
 468:         position.setExitReason(profitLock.explanation());
 469:         position.setExitSignal(signal);
 470:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 471:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
 472: 
 473:         // FIX-020: terminal position close consumes the opportunity that financed this
 474:         // position so old pre-exit evidence cannot immediately open a brand-new trade.
 475:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), signal, "PROFIT_LOCK");
 476: 
 477:         try {
 478:             walletAutoExecutionService.executeProfitLock(signal, exitPrice, profitLock.lockPrice());
 479:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 480:             log.error("Dynamic Profit Lock wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
 481:         }
 482:         // FIX-028: profit-lock exits use the same immutable production audit trail.
 483:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, signal, "PROFIT_LOCK", profitLock.explanation());
 484:         return saved;
 485:     }
 486: 
 487:     private PaperPosition completeClose(
 488:             PaperPosition position,
 489:             BigDecimal exitPrice,
 490:             PositionStatus status,
 491:             String closeReason,
 492:             String explanation,
 493:             TradeSignal exitSignal
 494:     ) {
 495:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
 496:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 497:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
 498:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
 499:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 500: 
 501:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 502:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 503:         position.setStatus(status);
 504:         position.setCloseReason(closeReason);
 505:         position.setExitReason(explanation);
 506:         position.setExitSignal(exitSignal);
 507:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 508:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
 509: 
 510:         // FIX-020: all terminal exits (TP/SL/SIGNAL_SELL/manual/etc.) create an immutable
 511:         // evidence boundary. Progressive adds remain untouched while the position is open.
 512:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), exitSignal, closeReason);
 513: 
 514:         if (exitSignal != null) {
 515:             try {
 516:                 // FIX-028: keep execution behavior identical, but preserve the TRUE terminal
 517:                 // reason in wallet history. A TAKE_PROFIT/STOP_LOSS may use the latest 1m
 518:                 // signal as its price/context carrier even when that signal is WATCH. Calling
 519:                 // executeSell(...) used to mislabel those exits as SIGNAL_SELL.
 520:                 if ("SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason) || "STRONG_SELL".equalsIgnoreCase(closeReason)) {
 521:                     walletAutoExecutionService.executeSell(exitSignal);
 522:                 } else {
 523:                     walletAutoExecutionService.executeSignalLinkedExit(exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 524:                 }
 525:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 526:                 log.error("Automatic wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", exitSignal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
 527:             }
 528:         }
 529: 
 530:         // FIX-028: immutable production audit records the actual close trigger separately
 531:         // from the latest market signal. This is diagnostic only and cannot alter execution.
 532:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, exitSignal, closeReason, explanation);
 533:         return saved;
 534:     }
 535: 
 536:     private String normalizeSymbol(String symbol) {
 537:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) {
 538:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Symbol is required");
 539:         }
 540:         return symbol.trim().toUpperCase();
 541:     }

 543:     private void enforceDailyLossLimit() {
 544:         Instant startOfDay = LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC).atStartOfDay().toInstant(ZoneOffset.UTC);
 545:         BigDecimal pnl = positionRepository.sumRealizedPnlSince(startOfDay);
 546:         BigDecimal maximumLoss = properties.paperAccountBalance()
 547:                 .multiply(properties.maxDailyLossPercent(), MC)
 548:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 549:                 .negate();

  65:     @Transactional
  66:     public boolean executeBuy(TradeSignal signal, int positionPercent, String executionExplanation,
  67:                                            String entryStage, int entryQualityScore) {
  68:         // FIX-056 compatibility overload. Normal Production/Replay paths MUST call the
  69:         // explicit-price overload below; retaining this overload avoids breaking admin/tests.
  70:         return executeBuy(signal, signal == null ? null : signal.getLatestPrice(), Instant.now(), positionPercent,
  71:                 executionExplanation, entryStage, entryQualityScore);

  73: 
  74:     /**
  75:      * FIX-056: execute using an explicit execution-time market price. TradeSignal.latestPrice
  76:      * is the immutable decision snapshot and must not be reused as the wallet fill.
  77:      */
  78:     @Transactional
  79:     public boolean executeBuy(TradeSignal signal, BigDecimal executionPrice, Instant executionPriceObservedAt, int positionPercent,
  80:                                            String executionExplanation, String entryStage, int entryQualityScore) {
  81:         if (signal == null || signal.getId() == null) return false;
  82:         int requestedPositionPercent = Math.max(1, Math.min(100, positionPercent));
  83: 
  84:         String pair = normalizePair(signal.getSymbol());
  85:         if(walletCoordination!=null) { walletCoordination.symbol(pair); }
  86:         WalletManagedPosition existingPosition = managedPositionRepository
  87:                 .findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(pair, "OPEN")
  88:                 .orElse(null);
  89:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.mutation();
  90:         // FIX-125: position precedes mutation; statistics precede cash on every exit.
  91:         dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC));
  92: 
  93:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  94:         int currentAllocatedPercent = existingPosition == null ? 0 : Math.max(0, existingPosition.getAllocatedPositionPercent());
  95: 
  96:         String key = signal.getId() + ":BUY";
  97:         if (executionExists(key)) return true;
  98: 
  99:         String assetSymbol = pair.substring(0, pair.length() - 4);
 100:         BigDecimal price = positive(executionPrice);
 101:         WalletAsset usdt = getOrCreate("USDT");
 102:         WalletDailyStatistics daily = dailyStatistics(settings, usdt);
 103: 
 104:         boolean newPosition = existingPosition == null;
 105:         WalletExecutionSizingPolicy.Plan sizing = executionSizingPolicy.plan(
 106:                 usdt.getQuantity(), settings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(), daily.getDailyTradeBudgetUsdt(),
 107:                 requestedPositionPercent, currentAllocatedPercent, newPosition,
 108:                 daily.getMaximumNewPositions(), daily.getExecutedBuys(), price);
 109:         if (!sizing.allowed()) return false;
 110:         int normalizedPositionPercent = sizing.normalizedPositionPercent();
 111:         BigDecimal spend = sizing.spend();
 112:         BigDecimal quantity = sizing.quantity();
 113: 
 114:         // FIX-037: debit USDT atomically before mutating the purchased asset. A stale
 115:         // Java-side WalletAsset instance must never overwrite a concurrent SELL credit.
 116:         if (assetRepository.debitQuantityIfSufficient("USDT", spend) != 1) return false;
 117:         if(walletCoordination!=null)getOrCreate("USDT");
 118:         WalletAsset coin = getOrCreate(assetSymbol);
 119:         BigDecimal oldCost = coin.getQuantity().multiply(nvl(coin.getAverageBuyPriceUsdt()));
 120:         BigDecimal newQuantity = coin.getQuantity().add(quantity);
 121:         coin.setQuantity(newQuantity);
 122:         coin.setAverageBuyPriceUsdt(oldCost.add(spend)
 123:                 .divide(newQuantity, SCALE, RoundingMode.HALF_UP));
 124:         assetRepository.save(coin);
 125:         // clearAutomatically on the atomic update invalidates the stale persistence context;
 126:         // reload USDT so statistics/snapshots use the committed post-debit balance.
 127:         usdt = getOrCreate("USDT");
 128: 
 129:         WalletManagedPosition position = existingPosition != null
 130:                 ? existingPosition
 131:                 : newManagedPosition(pair, signal, price);
 132:         position.setQuantity(position.getQuantity().add(quantity));
 133:         position.setTotalCostUsdt(position.getTotalCostUsdt().add(spend));
 134:         position.setAverageEntryPriceUsdt(position.getTotalCostUsdt()
 135:                 .divide(position.getQuantity(), SCALE, RoundingMode.HALF_UP));
 136:         position.setAllocatedPositionPercent(Math.min(100,
 137:                 position.getAllocatedPositionPercent() + normalizedPositionPercent));
 138:         position.setEntryStage(entryStage == null || entryStage.isBlank() ? "ENTRY_BUY" : entryStage);
 139:         position.setEntryQualityScore(Math.max(0, entryQualityScore));
 140:         if (!newPosition) {
 141:             position.setLastScaleInAt(Instant.now());
 142:             // FIX-11T: a progressive add changes average entry / risk geometry. Restart the
 143:             // Near-TP observation window only if this position has not harvested already.
 144:             // After a harvest, keep the terminal Near-TP state and one-harvest lifetime cap.
 145:             if (!position.isNearTpHarvestUsed()) {
 146:                 position.setNearTpState(com.crypto.position.service.NearTpState.INACTIVE);
 147:                 position.setNearTpBestPrice(null);
 148:                 position.setNearTpBearishStreak(0);
 149:                 position.setNearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(null);
 150:             }
 151:             if (signal.getStopLoss() != null && signal.getStopLoss().signum() > 0
 152:                     && (position.getStopLossUsdt() == null || signal.getStopLoss().compareTo(position.getStopLossUsdt()) > 0)) {
 153:                 position.setStopLossUsdt(signal.getStopLoss());
 154:             }
 155:             if (signal.getTakeProfit() != null && signal.getTakeProfit().signum() > 0
 156:                     && (position.getTakeProfitUsdt() == null || signal.getTakeProfit().compareTo(position.getTakeProfitUsdt()) > 0)) {
 157:                 position.setTakeProfitUsdt(signal.getTakeProfit());
 158:             }
 159:         }
 160:         position.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 161:         managedPositionRepository.save(position);
 162: 
 163:         tradeRepository.save(WalletTrade.builder()
 164:                 .signal(signal)
 165:                 .executionKey(key)
 166:                 .symbol(pair)
 167:                 .side("BUY")
 168:                 .quantity(quantity)
 169:                 .priceUsdt(price)
 170:                 .decisionPriceUsdt(signal.getLatestPrice())
 171:                 .executionPriceObservedAt(executionPriceObservedAt)
 172:                 .grossAmountUsdt(spend)
 173:                 .feeUsdt(ZERO)
 174:                 .netAmountUsdt(spend)
 175:                 .executionType("PAPER_AUTO")
 176:                 .executionReason(entryStage == null || entryStage.isBlank() ? "ENTRY_BUY" : entryStage)
 177:                 .status("EXECUTED")
 178:                 .executedAt(Instant.now())
 179:                 .notes("Automatic wallet BUY using " + normalizedPositionPercent + "% of the configured BUY budget")
 180:                 .executionMessage("BUY decision from trade signal #" + signal.getId()
 181:                         + " (decisionPrice=" + signal.getLatestPrice() + ") applied to wallet for " + pair
 182:                         + " at executionPrice=" + price + " and " + normalizedPositionPercent
 183:                         + "% size. " + (executionExplanation == null ? "" : executionExplanation))
 184:                 .build());
 185: 
 186:         if (newPosition) {
 187:             daily.setExecutedBuys(daily.getExecutedBuys() + 1);
 188:         }
 189:         daily.setEndingUsdt(usdt.getQuantity());
 190:         daily.setEndingPortfolioUsdt(walletService.currentPortfolioValue());
 191:         daily.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 192:         dailyStatisticsRepository.save(daily);
 193:         walletService.captureSnapshot();
 194:         return true;
 195:     }

 197:     @Transactional
 198:     public void executeSell(TradeSignal signal) {
 199:         executeSignalLinkedExit(signal, "SIGNAL_SELL",
 200:                 signal == null || signal.getId() == null ? "Signal SELL"
 201:                         : "SELL decision from trade signal #" + signal.getId() + " applied to wallet for " + normalizePair(signal.getSymbol()));

 204:     /**
 205:      * FIX-028: execute the same signal-linked wallet liquidation while preserving the
 206:      * position engine's actual terminal reason. This method intentionally keeps the same
 207:      * idempotency key (signalId:SELL), quantities, balances and close mechanics as the
 208:      * legacy executeSell path; only audit metadata is corrected.
 209:      */
 210:     @Transactional
 211:     public void executeSignalLinkedExit(TradeSignal signal, String executionReason, String executionMessage) {
 212:         if (signal == null || signal.getId() == null) return;
 213:         String key = signal.getId() + ":SELL";
 214:         String pair = normalizePair(signal.getSymbol());
 215:         if(walletCoordination!=null) { walletCoordination.symbol(pair); }
 216:         WalletManagedPosition position = managedPositionRepository.findFirstBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(pair, "OPEN").orElse(null);
 217:         if(walletCoordination!=null)walletCoordination.mutation();
 218:         // FIX-125: position precedes mutation; statistics precede cash on every exit.
 219:         dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC));
 220:         if (executionExists(key)) return;
 221: 
 222:         if (position == null || position.getQuantity().signum() <= 0) return;
 223: 
 224:         String assetSymbol = pair.substring(0, pair.length() - 4);
 225:         if(walletCoordination!=null)getOrCreate("USDT");
 226:         WalletAsset coin = getOrCreate(assetSymbol);
 227:         BigDecimal quantity = position.getQuantity().min(coin.getQuantity());
 228:         if (quantity.signum() <= 0) return;
 229: 
 230:         BigDecimal price = positive(signal.getLatestPrice());
 231:         BigDecimal gross = quantity.multiply(price);
 232:         BigDecimal costBasis = position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt().multiply(quantity);
 233:         BigDecimal realized = gross.subtract(costBasis);
 234:         BigDecimal realizedPercent = costBasis.signum() == 0 ? ZERO : realized
 235:                 .divide(costBasis, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
 236: 
 237:         coin.setQuantity(coin.getQuantity().subtract(quantity));
 238:         if (coin.getQuantity().signum() == 0) coin.setAverageBuyPriceUsdt(null);
 239:         assetRepository.save(coin);
 240:         // FIX-037: credit SELL proceeds atomically so a concurrent BUY cannot overwrite them.
 241:         if (assetRepository.creditQuantity("USDT", gross) != 1) {
 242:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to credit USDT wallet balance");
 243:         }
 244:         // FIX-037: keep a final post-credit balance snapshot for lambda-based daily-stat updates.
 245:         // Using a separate final variable also avoids capturing a reassigned local variable.
 246:         final BigDecimal endingUsdt = getOrCreate("USDT").getQuantity();
 247: 
 248:         position.setQuantity(ZERO);
 249:         position.setTotalCostUsdt(ZERO);
 250:         position.setAverageEntryPriceUsdt(ZERO);
 251:         position.setStatus("CLOSED");
 252:         position.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 253:         managedPositionRepository.save(position);
 254: 
 255:         String reason = executionReason == null || executionReason.isBlank()
 256:                 ? "SIGNAL_SELL" : executionReason.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
 257:         tradeRepository.save(WalletTrade.builder()
 258:                 .signal(signal).executionKey(key).symbol(pair).side("SELL")
 259:                 .quantity(quantity).priceUsdt(price).grossAmountUsdt(gross)
 260:                 .feeUsdt(ZERO).netAmountUsdt(gross).costBasisUsdt(costBasis)
 261:                 .realizedPnlUsdt(realized).realizedPnlPercent(realizedPercent)
 262:                 .executionType("PAPER_AUTO").executionReason(reason)
 263:                 .status("EXECUTED").executedAt(Instant.now())
 264:                 .notes("Automatic paper exit from persisted position trigger")
 265:                 .executionMessage(executionMessage == null || executionMessage.isBlank()
 266:                         ? reason + " executed using trade signal #" + signal.getId() + " as market context"
 267:                         : executionMessage)
 268:                 .build());
 269: 
 270:         dailyStatisticsRepository.findForUpdateByTradeDate(LocalDate.now(ZoneOffset.UTC))
 271:                 .ifPresent(daily -> {
 272:                     daily.setEndingUsdt(endingUsdt);
 273:                     daily.setEndingPortfolioUsdt(walletService.currentPortfolioValue());
 274:                     daily.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 275:                     dailyStatisticsRepository.save(daily);
 276:                 });
 277:         walletService.captureSnapshot();
 278:     }

  24: /**
  25:  * Position manager for positions already owned by the automatic wallet.
  26:  *
  27:  * It compares the immutable BUY thesis stored on WalletManagedPosition with the
  28:  * latest TradeSignal. It does not recalculate indicators or change the market
  29:  * decision. HOLD, REDUCE, TAKE_PROFIT and EXIT remain advisory-only in v1.
  30:  * A hard STOP_LOSS may be applied to the wallet through WalletAutoExecutionService.
  31:  */
  32: @Service
  33: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  34: @Slf4j
  35: public class PositionManagementService {
  36: 
  37:     private static final BigDecimal ZERO = BigDecimal.ZERO;
  38: 
  39:     private final WalletManagedPositionRepository managedPositionRepository;
  40:     private final PositionAnalysisRepository analysisRepository;
  41:     private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
  42:     private final WalletAutoExecutionService walletAutoExecutionService;
  43:     private final PositionPriceAuthorityPolicy priceAuthorityPolicy;
  44:     private final PositionThesisPressurePolicy thesisPressurePolicy;
  45: 
  46:     @Transactional
  47:     public Optional<PositionAnalysis> analyze(TradeSignal signal) {
  48:         if (!validSignal(signal)) return Optional.empty();
  49: 
  50:         String symbol = signal.getSymbol().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  51:         WalletManagedPosition position = managedPositionRepository
  52:                 .findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
  53:                 .orElse(null);
  54:         if (!validPosition(position)) return Optional.empty();
  55: 
  56:         if (analysisRepository.existsByWalletPositionIdAndTradeSignalId(position.getId(), signal.getId())) {
  57:             return analysisRepository.findTopByWalletPositionIdOrderByAnalyzedAtDesc(position.getId());
  58:         }
  59: 
  60:         BigDecimal entry = nvl(position.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt());
  61:         BigDecimal price = signal.getLatestPrice();
  62:         BigDecimal pnl = price.subtract(entry).multiply(position.getQuantity());
  63:         BigDecimal pnlPercent = percentageChange(entry, price);
  64:         long holdingMinutes = Math.max(0,
  65:                 Duration.between(position.getOpenedAt(), Instant.now()).toMinutes());
  66: 
  67:         // A TradeSignal price can belong to a candle that closed BEFORE this position opened.
  68:         // ALLOUSDT incident 2026-08-17: a delayed 5m signal carried 0.2806 from the
  69:         // 18:15-18:19 candle and falsely stopped a position opened at 18:23 @ 0.2822.
  70:         // Such signals are still valid thesis/MTF context, but they are forbidden from
  71:         // driving price-based hard protection. Live TP/SL is handled by
  72:         // LivePositionProtectionService from the actual live Binance price stream.
  73:         boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(signal, position.getOpenedAt());
  74:         PositionRecommendation hardRecommendation = authoritativePrice
  75:                 ? hardRiskRecommendation(position, price)
  76:                 : null;
  77: 
  78:         PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure thesis = thesisPressurePolicy.evaluate(
  79:                 position.getEntryTrendScore(), position.getEntryStructureScore(),
  80:                 position.getEntryMomentumScore(), position.getEntryVolumeScore(),
  81:                 position.getEntryConfidence(), position.getEntryTotalScore(), signal);
  82:         int trendDeterioration = thesis.trendPressure();
  83:         int momentumExhaustion = thesis.momentumPressure();
  84:         int profitProtection = profitProtection(pnlPercent, thesis);
  85:         int riskEvents = authoritativePrice ? stopRiskPressure(position, price) : 0;
  86:         int opportunityCost = 0; // intentionally excluded from v1
  87:         int exitScore = Math.min(25, trendDeterioration + momentumExhaustion
  88:                 + profitProtection + riskEvents);
  89: 
  90:         int authority = timeframeAuthority(signal.getInterval());
  91:         PositionRecommendation recommendation = hardRecommendation != null
  92:                 ? hardRecommendation
  93:                 : recommendation(exitScore, profitProtection, pnlPercent, authority);
  94:         int confidence = confidence(exitScore, recommendation, hardRecommendation != null);
  95: 
  96:         List<String> evidence = evidence(position, signal, pnlPercent, thesis,
  97:                 trendDeterioration, momentumExhaustion, profitProtection,
  98:                 riskEvents, authority, hardRecommendation);
  99:         String explanation = recommendation + " advisory: " + String.join("; ", evidence);
 100: 
 101:         Map<String, Object> details = details(position, signal, pnlPercent, thesis,
 102:                 trendDeterioration, momentumExhaustion, profitProtection,
 103:                 riskEvents, opportunityCost, exitScore, authority, evidence);
 104: 
 105:         PositionAnalysis saved = analysisRepository.save(PositionAnalysis.builder()
 106:                 .walletPosition(position)
 107:                 .tradeSignal(signal)
 108:                 .symbol(symbol)
 109:                 .intervalCode(signal.getInterval())
 110:                 .entryPriceUsdt(entry)
 111:                 .currentPriceUsdt(price)
 112:                 .unrealizedPnlUsdt(pnl)
 113:                 .unrealizedPnlPercent(pnlPercent)
 114:                 .holdingMinutes(holdingMinutes)
 115:                 .trendDeteriorationScore(trendDeterioration)
 116:                 .momentumExhaustionScore(momentumExhaustion)
 117:                 .profitProtectionScore(profitProtection)
 118:                 .riskEventScore(riskEvents)
 119:                 .opportunityCostScore(opportunityCost)
 120:                 .exitScore(exitScore)
 121:                 .recommendation(recommendation)
 122:                 .confidence(confidence)
 123:                 .explanation(limit(explanation, 2000))
 124:                 .detailsJson(toJson(details))
 125:                 .advisoryOnly(true)
 126:                 .analyzedAt(Instant.now())
 127:                 .build());
 128: 
 129:         if (recommendation == PositionRecommendation.STOP_LOSS) {

 131:             if (applied) {

 133:                 details.put("advisoryOnly", false);

 136:                 saved.setDetailsJson(toJson(details));
 137:                 saved = analysisRepository.save(saved);
 138:                 log.warn("Position STOP_LOSS applied to wallet: position={}, analysis={}, signal={}, symbol={}",
 139:                         position.getId(), saved.getId(), signal.getId(), symbol);
 140:             } else {

 168:     private PositionRecommendation hardRiskRecommendation(WalletManagedPosition p, BigDecimal price) {
 169:         if (p.getStopLossUsdt() != null && p.getStopLossUsdt().signum() > 0
 170:                 && price.compareTo(p.getStopLossUsdt()) <= 0) {
 171:             return PositionRecommendation.STOP_LOSS;
 172:         }
 173:         if (p.getTakeProfitUsdt() != null && p.getTakeProfitUsdt().signum() > 0
 174:                 && price.compareTo(p.getTakeProfitUsdt()) >= 0) {
 175:             return PositionRecommendation.TAKE_PROFIT;
 176:         }
 177:         return null;
 178:     }
 179: 
 180:     private int profitProtection(BigDecimal pnlPercent, PositionThesisPressurePolicy.ThesisPressure thesis) {
 181:         if (pnlPercent.signum() <= 0) return 0;
 182:         int score = 0;
 183:         if (pnlPercent.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(5)) >= 0) score += 2;
 184:         else if (pnlPercent.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(2)) >= 0) score += 1;
 185:         if (thesis.trendDrop() >= 6 || thesis.structureDrop() >= 3) score += 2;
 186:         if (thesis.momentumDrop() >= 5 || thesis.totalDrop() >= 15) score += 1;
 187:         return Math.min(5, score);
 188:     }
 189: 
 190:     private int stopRiskPressure(WalletManagedPosition p, BigDecimal price) {
 191:         BigDecimal stop = p.getStopLossUsdt();
 192:         BigDecimal entry = p.getAverageEntryPriceUsdt();
 193:         if (stop == null || stop.signum() <= 0 || entry == null || entry.compareTo(stop) <= 0) return 0;
 194:         BigDecimal totalRoom = entry.subtract(stop);
 195:         BigDecimal remaining = price.subtract(stop);
 196:         if (remaining.signum() <= 0) return 4;
 197:         BigDecimal ratio = remaining.divide(totalRoom, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 198:         if (ratio.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.25)) <= 0) return 3;
 199:         if (ratio.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50)) <= 0) return 1;
 200:         return 0;
 201:     }
 202: 
 203:     private PositionRecommendation recommendation(int exitScore, int profitProtection,
 204:                                                   BigDecimal pnlPercent, int authority) {
 205:         // A 1m advisory can warn or recommend reduction, but cannot independently
 206:         // invalidate the full position thesis.
 207:         if (authority >= 2 && exitScore >= 17) return PositionRecommendation.EXIT;
 208:         if (authority >= 2 && pnlPercent.signum() > 0
 209:                 && profitProtection >= 4 && exitScore >= 10) {
 210:             return PositionRecommendation.TAKE_PROFIT;
 211:         }
 212:         if (exitScore >= 10) return PositionRecommendation.REDUCE;
 213:         return PositionRecommendation.HOLD;
 214:     }
 215: 
 216:     private int timeframeAuthority(String interval) {
 217:         if (interval == null) return 1;
 218:         return switch (interval.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT)) {
 219:             case "1h", "4h", "1d" -> 3;
 220:             case "5m", "15m", "30m" -> 2;
 221:             default -> 1;
 222:         };
 223:     }
 224: 
 225:     private int confidence(int score, PositionRecommendation recommendation, boolean hardTrigger) {
 226:         if (hardTrigger) return 95;
 227:         int base = switch (recommendation) {
 228:             case HOLD -> 85 - Math.min(30, score * 3);
 229:             case REDUCE -> 65 + Math.min(20, Math.abs(score - 13) * 3);
 230:             case TAKE_PROFIT -> 75 + Math.min(15, score);
 231:             case STOP_LOSS -> 95;
 232:             case EXIT -> 75 + Math.min(20, score - 16);
 233:         };
 234:         return Math.max(50, Math.min(95, base));
 235:     }

 258:         if (risk > 0) e.add("Price is approaching the stored stop-loss; risk pressure " + risk + "/4");
